## Original request

Preserve distinct workspace and verification bases

Keep the original workspace base separate from the current comparison base in Work artifacts and downstream publication inputs.

## Why

The final Work response declares comparison base 02a67f6, while its saved candidateBase and Publish PR input contain d143a6f. Delivery verification uses 02a67f6. This discrepancy makes the audit trail ambiguous.

## Verification

Cover a branch update followed by a no-change Work result. Confirm the supplied comparison base survives artifact recording and downstream consumption, with the original workspace base separately labeled.

## Source issue

20261120T075006795-d932ecc8feef2b4f
